HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"quarantine_without_hft","docket_ready":true}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":["root_000047/B002","root_001567/B001","root_001567/B002","root_001567/B006"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_5421d4e3c9274e7aa601","text":"2. **The she-camel as elevated, selected sign.** Lexical evidence: ن و ق B002 \"she-camel and its shape\"; ن و ق B001 \"elevation and height\"; ن و ق B006 \"choosing or selecting\"; ء ل ه B002 \"Allah as fixed name.\" Structural cue: the noun is directly annexed to Allah, paralleling `رسول الله`. Assigned role: ناقة is not generic livestock but an elevated/selected creature marked by divine belonging. Abductive move: I infer that genitive attribution turns her into a sign with inviolable status; the packet supplies she-camel, elevation/selection, and divine name. Reading change: \"naqat Allah\" becomes a protected selected presence, so harming her is rebellion against the ordering name, not property damage.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000722/B002","root_001567/B004"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-4","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_71dd86312d82ba4597c4","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"91:13:5\",\"root_001567\",[\"B004\"]],[\"91:13:7\",\"root_000722\",[\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Deveyi gözetip işleri yoluna koyma görevi, bu düzeni yaralayarak egemen olmak yerine suya erişimi ölçülü biçimde yöneten bir sorumluluk sınavına dönüşür.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000047/B002","root_001567/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_7e11623c4b592e886179","text":"4. **Legitimate elevation opposed to false ascent.** **Lexical evidence:** fixed `ن و ق/B001` carries elevation and the mountain's highest point; the she-camel is twice enclosed by the divine name through fixed `ء ل ه/B002`. **Structural cue and role:** 91:11-12 activate transgressive high ground and a rising superlative, whereas this ayah elevates the animal by attributed ownership before 91:14 levels the violators. **Abductive move:** status elevation is inferred from the root branch and genitive framing; no physical ascent of the animal is stated. **Reading change:** the protected she-camel occupies the legitimate high claim against the group's counterfeit prominence.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000047/B002","root_000563/B002","root_001272/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_8075c2b97155bbc5362a","text":"1. **Messenger speech as boundary-setting utterance.** Lexical evidence: ق و ل B001 \"uttered speech\"; ر س ل B002 \"messenger and message\"; ء ل ه B002 fixed Allah-name; و ق ي B001/B002 protection/caution in 91:8. Structural cue: the messenger's saying appears between the roused wretched one and the hamstringing. Assigned role: the utterance installs a protective boundary around \"she-camel of Allah\" and \"her watering.\" Abductive move: I infer that the elliptical phrase functions as warning/preservation; the packet supplies speech, messenger, divine attribution, and protective root nearby. Reading change: the ayah reads less as bare quotation and more as a verbal fence drawn around a creature and its water-right.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000722/B002","root_001567/B004"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_84a25aaab6bf1939d54e","text":"3. **A test of management rather than domination.** **Lexical evidence:** fixed `ن و ق/B004` is taming, training, and setting affairs right; fixed `س ق ي/B002` is providing access to a water source. **Structural cue and role:** a bounded animal-water arrangement is placed before the collective's disabling act, so management means maintaining access and restraint rather than mastering by injury. **Abductive move:** the arrangement and later violation are explicit; construing them as a governance test is inferred. **Reading change:** `ناقة الله وسقياها` becomes a compact social order whose success depends on disciplined stewardship.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001272/B007","root_001272/B014","root_001290/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_8e41d46d55e7404e2d4a","text":"4. **Speech as public report against circulating falsehood.** Lexical evidence: ق و ل B007 \"circulating talk or public report\"; ك ذ ب B001/B002 falsehood/declaring false in 91:11 and 91:14; ق و ل B014 \"thing's saying as indication.\" Structural cue: the messenger's قول is surrounded by takdhib. Assigned role: true speech and sign-indication confront false public talk. Abductive move: I infer that the she-camel herself \"says\" something as signification while the messenger verbalizes it; the packet supplies saying, indication, and repeated denial. Reading change: the ayah reads as a layered communication event: word plus sign plus public counter-speech.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000722/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_913ff852a48a75f20a78","text":"3. **Watering as social right and ecological share.** Lexical evidence: س ق ي B001 \"giving drink\"; B002 \"providing a watering source\"; B003 \"irrigation share and channel\"; ذ ن ب B006 \"portion or allotted share\" in 91:14. Structural cue: `وسقياها` is paired with the she-camel, then their sin/consequence follows. Assigned role: watering is the material right attached to the protected creature. Abductive move: I infer that the conflict is over allocation and access, not merely belief; the packet supplies watering-source/share and later sin/share language. Reading change: the ayah becomes a command about respecting a divinely assigned ecological/social share.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000563/B002","root_001272/B007","root_001272/B014"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-5","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_929141acad9c88d727da","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"91:13:1\",\"root_001272\",[\"B007\",\"B014\"]],[\"91:13:3\",\"root_000563\",[\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Elçinin kamusal sözü ile devenin işaret oluşu birlikte, topluluğun dolaşıma soktuğu yalanlamaya hem sözlü hem görünür bir karşı bildirim oluşturur.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000047/B002","root_001567/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-6","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_99578e82d9634dd98239","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"91:13:4\",\"root_000047\",[\"B002\"]],[\"91:13:5\",\"root_001567\",[\"B001\"]],[\"91:13:6\",\"root_000047\",[\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"reject\",\"text\":\"Devenin yüksekliği, azgın topluluğun sahte yükselişine karşı ilahi aidiyetle meşrulaşan üstün bir talep gibi okunabilir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001272/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_b38d8652584739c4f93b","text":"1. **Speech as notice of a protected resource relation.** **Lexical evidence:** fixed `ق و ل/B001` is articulated speech, fixed `ر س ل/B002` is messenger and carried message, and fixed `س ق ي/B003` is an irrigation share or channel. **Structural cue and role:** `قال لهم` introduces the compressed nominal pair `ناقة الله وسقياها`; the possessive suffix gives watering an attached entitlement, so the utterance functions as boundary notice. **Abductive move:** words, messenger, animal, and water share are supplied; taking the ellipsis as warning or injunction is the inferred performative force. **Reading change:** the saying does not merely identify an animal; it publicly establishes a protected allocation.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_bd960396c3784977e776","text":"91:14 made the messenger's clipped saying heavier: `ناقة الله وسقياها` functions as a boundary whose violation is immediately narrated. 91:15 then made the speech legally final in retrospect: their denial challenges the messenger's public word, but the Lord's aftermath admits no follow-up challenge.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000563/B006","root_000722/B001","root_001567/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-3","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_c027f11f27a9b1051ba3","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"91:13:3\",\"root_000563\",[\"B006\"]],[\"91:13:5\",\"root_001567\",[\"B001\"]],[\"91:13:7\",\"root_000722\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Elçinin bildirisi, su verilen canlının süt ve diğer yararlarının kesintisiz sürmesini koruyan bir yaşam düzenini de taşır.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000563/B004"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_c69024f220679318780b","text":"91:14 reveals that the compressed utterance was consequential notice: denying the messenger and hamstringing the animal violate its two named poles. The rapid *fa-* chain also sharpens the contrast between `ر س ل/B004` deliberate restraint and domination by impulsive injury.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000047/B002","root_001567/B001","root_001567/B006"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_c7228700038a9c5ab026","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"91:13:4\",\"root_000047\",[\"B002\"]],[\"91:13:5\",\"root_001567\",[\"B001\",\"B006\"]],[\"91:13:6\",\"root_000047\",[\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Devenin Allah'a ait diye belirlenmesi, onu sıradan bir maldan ayırıp seçilmiş ve dokunulmaz bir işaret konumuna yükseltir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000563/B002","root_000722/B003","root_001272/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_cab9317595cedc6474ad","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"91:13:1\",\"root_001272\",[\"B001\"]],[\"91:13:3\",\"root_000563\",[\"B002\"]],[\"91:13:7\",\"root_000722\",[\"B003\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Elçinin kısa sözü, deve ile ona ayrılan su payının çevresine kamusal ve koruyucu bir sınır çizen bağlayıcı bir bildirimdir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000563/B006"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_f6d68d427ca97fd7fdbd","text":"2. **A message that safeguards continuous yield.** **Lexical evidence:** fixed `ر س ل/B006` is milk and flowing yield, fixed `ن و ق/B002` is the she-camel, and fixed `س ق ي/B001` is giving drink. **Structural cue and role:** messenger, she-camel, and watering occur in one compact utterance, while denial and hamstringing follow; the dormant milk branch makes the message the carrier of a life-sustaining flow. **Abductive move:** the lexical materials are supplied, but inferring milk or wider provision as the protected yield is material analogy, not an explicit statement. **Reading change:** the messenger carries a resource-preservation claim as well as verbal content.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"anchor_evidence":[],"anchor_evidence_coverage":{"boundary":"Exact Arabic verifies surface contact only.
